### Identity (reviewed)

Preserves the John Hall Mine Storm source and adapts the Avocet ASM09 input to the Kingswood as09 assembler; main_loop.asm implements initialization, minefield sequencing and scoring rather than merely wrapping a binary.
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/README.md
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/Makefile
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/mine-storm-devel.asm
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/main_loop.asm

### Classification (reviewed)

The README attributes the released source to John Hall and explains that this is a split, commented learning adaptation rather than a stock Rev C reconstruction. No standalone game license appears in the pinned tree; the included assembler has its own terms, which do not establish rights for the game.
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/README.md
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/Makefile
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/mine-storm-devel.asm
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/main_loop.asm

### Source CPU (reviewed)

The restored input is native Vectrex 6809 assembly: direct-page setup and A/B/X/Y/U/S register operations are present in the reviewed game loop.
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/README.md
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/Makefile
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/mine-storm-devel.asm
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/main_loop.asm

### Target CPU (reviewed)

The intended cartridge output is Motorola 6809, established by the native register/instruction code and Vectrex BIOS calls.
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/README.md
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/Makefile
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/mine-storm-devel.asm
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/main_loop.asm

### Build (reviewed)

Makefile invokes the bundled as09_142/as09 against mine-storm-devel.asm, which includes the equates, RAM, initialization, main-loop and subroutine files. The upstream author reports success, but this review did not execute the assembler or compare output bytes.
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/README.md
- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/Makefile

- https://github.com/mikepea/vectrex-minestorm/blob/67a6ce7de214c8d1894854cd0655bab0dff08798/main_loop.asm

### Relationships (reviewed)

This adaptation derives from John Hall’s released Mine Storm source, not a new independent disassembly. The local catalogue has other Vectrex shooters, including MineX, but no exact repository/source-adaptation record.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Vectrex Hexpawn implementation by Dan Siewers, inspired by Robert C. Leedom’s KIM Hexpawn. Source/main.asm implements cartridge startup, a 3×3 pawn board, move state and learned-response storage; a separate Binary/Vexpawn.bin is supplied.

## Decisions

```json
[
  {
    "decision": "deferred",
    "evidence_urls": [
      "https://github.com/nlifors/New_Zealand_Story/blob/e6a05643d7095ad1f2cacbc605362b28b292eab3/README.md",
      "https://github.com/nlifors/New_Zealand_Story/blob/e6a05643d7095ad1f2cacbc605362b28b292eab3/Sources/NZSRecomp/Translator.swift",
      "https://github.com/nlifors/New_Zealand_Story/blob/e6a05643d7095ad1f2cacbc605362b28b292eab3/Sources/NZSGame/Runtime.swift"
    ],
    "id": "2026-10-19-defer-nzs-swift-translation",
    "project_ids": [],
    "reason": "The README describes build-time static translation of original 68000 routines into generated Swift with original memory/register/timing behaviour and an Amiga hardware runtime. The M68K interpreter is used for reference/lockstep checks, so this is not described as merely running the game through that interpreter. Generated game code is not committed. Deferred pending review of whether the maintained game-specific translator/runtime qualifies under the current policy, or offers independently reusable tooling; no high-level recovered game-source promotion is made.",
    "source_ids": [
      "wide-20261019u-nzs-swift-static-translation-review"
    ],
    "title": "The New Zealand Story Swift translation scope",
    "url": "https://github.com/nlifors/New_Zealand_Story"
  }
]
```

## Research event notes

Pass21: deduplicated primary-source research. Existing maccraft/elite-mac/scaffold deferrals retained; no empty templates or binary-only packages promoted; the game-specific static-translation lead is explicitly deferred for scope/policy review. Independent build/run and byte matching were not performed.
